# 6.1.1 涵蓋問題：涵蓋不足、過度涵蓋、重疊涵蓋

所屬：[6.1 根因的類型](6-1.md)

## 內文

#### 是什麼、什麼時候會用到

涵蓋問題是指小區的無線訊號「到不了該到的地方」，或「到了不該到的地方」。本節分成三類：**涵蓋不足**（含訊號偏弱的 weak coverage 與完全沒有可用訊號的 coverage hole）、**過度涵蓋**（overshoot）、**重疊涵蓋**（多個小區訊號強度相近、沒有主導小區，TS 37.320 稱為 pilot pollution）。TS 37.320 V18.5.0 Annex A 把 coverage hole 描述為服務小區與允許的鄰區 SNR（或 SINR）都低於維持基本服務所需水準的區域，把 overshoot 描述為小區涵蓋遠超出規劃範圍，可能在別的小區內部形成「孤島」（[TS 37.320](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/37320-i50.zip)）。常見情境：客服收到新大樓室內收不到訊號的客訴；某小區無線鏈路失敗（RLF）與掉話上升；新站開通或天線調整後，遠處出現奇怪的換手對象。

#### 怎麼做／怎麼判斷

**1. 先把三個量的定義讀對（TS 38.215 V18.4.0，[TS 38.215](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.215/38215-i40.zip)）。**

- SS-RSRP：承載輔同步訊號（SSS）的資源元素上功率的線性平均（clause 5.1.1）。它只反映「有用訊號多強」，不含干擾。
- SS-SINR：承載 SSS 的資源元素功率的線性平均，除以雜訊加干擾功率的線性平均（clause 5.1.5），適用於 RRC_CONNECTED。
- 時間提前量 Timing advance（TADV）＝ TgNB-RX − TgNB-TX，由基地台以 UE 送出的 PRACH、取最早偵測到的路徑來量（clause 5.2.7）。實務報表看的是 gNB 送出的 Absolute Timing Advance Command 統計，量測名 `L1M.ATADist.Bin`（TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.33.1）。TS 38.213 V18.9.0 clause 4.2 規定這類命令的索引 TA＝0～3846，對應 NTA＝TA·16·64/2^μ（以 Tc 為單位，Tc 見 TS 38.211 V18.10.0 clause 4.1）。TA 是來回時間，換成距離要除以 2：子載波間隔 30 kHz（μ＝1）時，每一階約 39.06 m；15 kHz 時約 78.13 m。

**2. 資料來源。** TS 28.552 V18.12.0（[TS 28.552](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/28552-ic0.zip)）可用的量測有：`L1M.SS-RSRP.Bin`（clause 5.1.1.22.1，以 SSB 波束為單位，需啟用 L1-RSRP 回報）、鄰區的 `L1M.SS-RSRPNrNbr.SSBIndex.Bin`（clause 5.1.1.22.2，掛在 NRCellRelation）、`MR.NRScSSRSRQ.BinX`（clause 5.1.1.31.1）、`MR.NRScSSSINR.BinX`（clause 5.1.1.32.1）。MDT 方面，Immediate MDT 的 M1 是服務小區與鄰區的下行訊號量（TS 37.320 V18.5.0 clause 5.4.1.1）；Logged MDT 在 NR 記錄 RSRP 與 RSRQ，UE 進入 any cell selection 狀態時至少記錄 `anyCellSelectionDetected`、時間戳與可用的位置資訊（clause 5.1.1.2），這是閒置 UE 掉進涵蓋破洞的直接證據。

**3. 三類問題的特徵。**

| 類型 | RSRP | SINR | TA／距離 | 站距與位置 | 重疊小區數 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 涵蓋不足 | 低 | 低（訊號弱，雜訊主導） | 多落在規劃半徑內或邊緣；室內深處的問題 TA 不一定大 | 站距過大、新建物遮蔽、地形 | 少，鄰區也弱 |
| 過度涵蓋 | 在規劃範圍外仍有可用強度 | 在別人的區域裡干擾別人 | 有一段明顯超過規劃半徑 | 站高、下傾角小、跨水面或建物反射 | 被非直接鄰區的 UE 量到 |
| 重疊涵蓋 | 中到高 | 低 | 正常 | 站距密 | 多個小區 RSRP 相近，沒有主導小區 |

**4. 和干擾、容量問題的區分。** RSRP 低、SINR 也低 → 先往涵蓋不足查。RSRP 正常、SINR 低 → 屬於干擾類：量測報告裡找得到幾個強度相近的自家小區，是重疊涵蓋；找不到對應小區，就轉 6.1.2 查外部干擾或 PCI 問題。RSRP、SINR、CQI 分布都沒變，卻在忙時 PRB 使用率高、速率下降 → 轉 6.1.4。涵蓋問題通常跟著「地點」走，不跟著「時段」走。

**5. 先排除 SON 的影響。** NG-RAN 節點可以自主在 OAM 配置的涵蓋組態之間調整與切換（TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.5.5.3）；集中式 CCO 可調的參數包括 configuredMaxTxPower、configuredMaxTxEIRP、coverageShape、digitalTilt、digitalAzimuth（TS 28.313 V18.2.0 Table 7.2.3.2.1-1），調整成功後，provisioning MnS 的提供者會送出屬性變更通知，其中 sourceIndicator＝SON_operation（clause 8.3.4；通知名稱 notifyMOIAttributeValueChanges 見 TS 28.532 V18.8.0 clause 11.1.1.9）。不過 TS 28.532 在同一通知的輸入參數說明中也提醒，提供者不一定知道變更來自 SON，此時 sourceIndicator 可能是別的值（clause 11.1.1.9.2）。看到涵蓋突然改變，先查這類通知，再懷疑硬體或人為改參數。

#### 實際例子

甲電信（虛構）南區 3.5 GHz NR 小區週報摘錄，SCS 30 kHz，數值全為虛構；RSRP、SINR 分段與「6 dB 內」的切法是業者自訂的報表門檻（虛構），不是規格值：

```
統計期間：2026-11-18 ~ 11-24，每日 18:00-22:00
小區          規劃半徑  SS-RSRP<-110dBm  SS-SINR<0dB  TA索引>51   與服務小區差6dB內  RRU.PrbDl
                        樣本比例         樣本比例     樣本比例    的鄰區數(中位數)   平均(%)
NRC-A101-1    600 m     41%              38%          2%          0                  22
NRC-B205-2    500 m      6%               9%         19%          1                  35
NRC-C330-3    400 m      3%              47%          1%          4                  40

補充：
- A101-1 扇區內 Logged MDT 有 27 筆 anyCellSelectionDetected，集中在 11-10 啟用的新大樓一帶
- B205-2 的遠距樣本，同時期鄰區量測出現 3.8 km 外、河對岸非相鄰小區 NRC-K880-1
```

逐步判斷：

1. 先換算 TA：30 kHz 時每階約 39.06 m（依 TS 38.213 V18.9.0 clause 4.2 的 NTA 式子，[TS 38.213](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.213/38213-i90.zip)），索引 51 約 1,992 m，所以「TA 索引>51」就是「超過約 2 km」。
2. **A101-1**：RSRP 低與 SINR 低的比例同時偏高，6 dB 內的鄰區數是 0，PRB 只用了 22%，符合涵蓋不足而不是干擾或容量；MDT 的 anyCellSelectionDetected 集中在新大樓周邊，指向遮蔽造成的涵蓋破洞。**不能下的結論**：還沒查告警與組態，不能排除射頻單元功率下降（6.1.7）或下傾角被改（6.1.6）。
3. **B205-2**：規劃半徑 500 m，卻有 19% 的 TA 樣本在 2 km 以外，加上河對岸非相鄰小區被量到，符合過度涵蓋。**不能下的結論**：TA 是最早路徑的來回時間，跨水面反射也會讓距離看起來變遠；要再比對組態歷史（含 SON_operation 通知）才知道是不是下傾角改變。
4. **C330-3**：RSRP 不差，SINR<0 dB 卻占 47%，中位數有 4 個鄰區落在 6 dB 內，符合重疊涵蓋，干擾來源是可辨識的自家小區。**不能下的結論**：同一區若還有上行參考訊號序列衝突（6.1.2），症狀會疊加，要另外檢查。

#### 容易搞混的地方

1. **只看 SINR 分不出涵蓋不足和干擾。** 兩者 SINR 都低，差別在 RSRP：弱涵蓋是訊號本身弱，干擾是訊號不弱但雜訊加干擾大（TS 38.215 V18.4.0 clause 5.1.1、5.1.5，[TS 38.215](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.215/38215-i40.zip)）。
2. **TA 不是精確距離。** TADV 取 PRACH 最早偵測到的路徑；非直視與反射會讓 TA 偏大，而靠近基地台的室內弱涵蓋 TA 反而很小，不能拿「TA 小」排除涵蓋問題。
3. **過度涵蓋不等於鄰區漏配。** 過度涵蓋常讓 UE 換到遠處小區或換手失敗，看起來像 6.1.3 的鄰區問題；前者是無線涵蓋超出規劃，後者是鄰區關係設定，兩者要分開查，也可能同時存在。
4. **小區多不一定是重疊涵蓋。** 有一個明顯主導的小區時，周邊小區多並不會讓 SINR 變差；問題在於幾個小區強度相近、沒有主導。

#### 和其他小節的關係

量測定義與換算見 5.1.7，MDT 資料見 5.1.5，基線比較見 5.4.1。干擾見 6.1.2，鄰區與換手見 6.1.3，容量見 6.1.4，下傾角與功率設定錯誤見 6.1.6，上行受限見 6.1.10。資料不足時用 MDT 補收（6.2.3）；調整下傾角或功率屬於需要批准的處置（6.3.2），走第 9 章異動流程。涵蓋特徵的量測依據見 [TS 28.552](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/28552-ic0.zip)。

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.215 V18.4.0 clause 5.1.1、5.1.5、5.2.7（[連結](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.215/38215-i40.zip)）；TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.22.1、5.1.1.22.2、5.1.1.31.1、5.1.1.32.1、5.1.1.33.1（[連結](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/28552-ic0.zip)）；TS 37.320 V18.5.0 clause 5.1.1.2、5.4.1.1、Annex A（[連結](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/37320-i50.zip)）；TS 38.213 V18.9.0 clause 4.2（[連結](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.213/38213-i90.zip)）；TS 38.211 V18.10.0 clause 4.1（[連結](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.211/38211-ia0.zip)）；TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.5.5（[連結](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/38300-ib0.zip)）；TS 28.313 V18.2.0 clause 6.4.2.4、7.2.3.2.1、8.3.4（[連結](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.313/28313-i20.zip)）；TS 28.532 V18.8.0 clause 11.1.1.9（[連結](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.532/28532-i80.zip)）

## 練習題（256 題）

### 補充練習題（85 題）

#### Reading RSRP and SINR together to separate weak coverage, interference and capacity

**情境 001**　Weekly busy-hour distributions for four cells

Telco-X (fictional) exported one week of busy-hour statistics for four 3.5 GHz NR cells. The thresholds (-115 dBm, 0 dB) are fictional operator report thresholds, not specification values.

```
Period: 2026-11-09 .. 2026-11-15, daily 18:00-22:00
Source: L1M.SS-RSRP.Bin (aggregated over all SSB beams), MR.NRScSSSINR.BinX,
        RRU.PrbDl, DRB.UEThpDl

Cell        SS-RSRP<-115dBm  SS-SINR<0dB  RRU.PrbDl  DRB.UEThpDl
            share of samples share        avg (%)    avg (Mbit/s)
HL-0207-1   36%              33%          18         21
HL-0207-2    4%              41%          37         26
HL-0311-1    5%               7%          93          9
HL-0311-3    3%               5%          30         95

Baseline (4 weeks before, same hours):
HL-0207-1   34%              31%          17         23
HL-0207-2    4%              12%          35         71
HL-0311-1    5%               6%          61         38
HL-0311-3    3%               5%          29         97
```

**001.** Which cell shows the signature that should first be investigated as a weak-coverage problem rather than an interference or capacity problem?

1. HL-0207-2, because it has the highest share of samples with SS-SINR below 0 dB
2. HL-0207-1, because a high share of low SS-RSRP samples comes together with a high share of low SS-SINR while the PRB load is low
3. HL-0311-1, because it has the lowest average DL UE throughput
4. HL-0311-3, because its throughput is far above the other cells and therefore its coverage must be shrinking

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　2

**詳解**　Low SS-RSRP together with low SS-SINR means the wanted signal itself is weak and noise dominates, which is the weak-coverage signature; the 18% PRB load rules out congestion. Option 1 is wrong because HL-0207-2 has normal RSRP (4% below threshold), so its low SINR points to interference, not weak signal. Option 3 is wrong because HL-0311-1's low throughput comes with 93% PRB usage and normal RSRP/SINR, a capacity signature. Option 4 is wrong because nothing in HL-0311-3's data indicates a problem.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.215 V18.4.0 clauses 5.1.1 and 5.1.5 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.215/); Curriculum 6.1.1 (characteristics table and "distinguishing from interference and capacity")

</details>

**002.** HL-0311-1 has normal SS-RSRP and SS-SINR shares, but its average RRU.PrbDl rose from 61% to 93% and DRB.UEThpDl fell from 38 to 9 Mbit/s. Which subsection's checks should be applied next?

1. 6.1.1 coverage problems, because throughput dropped by more than 75%
2. 6.1.2 interference, because a throughput drop always means the SINR is degraded
3. 6.1.6 parameter errors, because a downtilt change always shows up first as a PRB increase
4. 6.1.4 capacity and scheduling resources, because radio conditions are unchanged while PRB usage is near saturation

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　4

**詳解**　Unchanged RSRP and SINR distributions with PRB usage close to 100% and falling user throughput is the capacity signature, so the case moves to 6.1.4. Option 1 is wrong because coverage problems show up in the RSRP/SINR distributions, which did not change. Option 2 is wrong because the SINR share is 7% versus a 6% baseline, essentially unchanged. Option 3 is wrong because a tilt change would alter the RSRP distribution, and "always" is unjustified.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.2.1 (RRU.PrbDl) and clause 5.1.1.3.1 (DRB.UEThpDl) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/); Curriculum 6.1.1

</details>

**003.** According to TS 38.215, what does SS-SINR include in its denominator that SS-RSRP does not take into account at all?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　The linear average of the noise and interference power contribution; SS-RSRP is only the linear average of the power of the resource elements carrying secondary synchronization signals.

**詳解**　SS-RSRP (clause 5.1.1) measures only the received power on SSS resource elements, so it says how strong the wanted signal is. SS-SINR (clause 5.1.5) divides that power by the linear average of the noise and interference power, so it can be low either because the signal is weak or because interference is high. This is why SINR alone cannot separate weak coverage from interference.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.215 V18.4.0 clauses 5.1.1 and 5.1.5 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.215/)

</details>

**004.** The SS-RSRP column of the four-cell table was built from the TS 28.552 measurement L1M.SS-RSRP.Bin. Which statement about this TS 28.552 measurement is correct?

1. A per-SSB SS-RSRP distribution, collected when L1-RSRP reporting is enabled.
2. It is collected per NRCellRelation from neighbour-cell measurement reports
3. It is reported by idle-mode UEs through Logged MDT and therefore covers UEs that never connect
4. It is the distribution of absolute timing advance commands sent by the gNB

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　1

**詳解**　TS 28.552 clause 5.1.1.22.1 defines L1M.SS-RSRP.Bin as the distribution of SS-RSRP per SSB received from UEs when SS-RSRP is used for L1-RSRP and the L1-RSRP report function is enabled; its measured object is Beam. Option 2 describes L1M.SS-RSRPNrNbr.SSBIndex.Bin (clause 5.1.1.22.2, NRCellRelation). Option 3 is wrong because the counter is built from reports received by the gNB, not from idle-mode logs. Option 4 describes L1M.ATADist.Bin (clause 5.1.1.33.1).

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.22.1, 5.1.1.22.2 and 5.1.1.33.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**005.** HL-0207-2's SS-SINR<0 dB share rose from 12% to 41% with normal RSRP. Can you decide from this table whether the cause is overlapping coverage by own cells or external interference? If not, what is missing?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Cannot be determined: the table has no neighbour-cell measurements, so the number and strength of own neighbour cells close to the serving cell (e.g. from L1M.SS-RSRPNrNbr.SSBIndex.Bin or neighbour results in measurement reports) are needed to see whether identifiable own cells explain the low SINR.

**詳解**　Normal RSRP with low SINR places the case in the interference family. Overlapping coverage is recognised when the measurement reports contain several own cells of similar strength with no dominant cell; if no such cells are found, the case moves to external interference or PCI checks (6.1.2). The table only contains serving-cell distributions, so neither branch can be confirmed.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.22.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/); Curriculum 6.1.1 (distinguishing from interference)

</details>

**情境 003**　Overnight step change in one cell's RSRP distribution

Telco-X (fictional) noticed a step change in cell KH-0412-2. Neighbour cells KH-0412-1 and KH-0415-3 show no change over the same days.

```
Cell KH-0412-2, daily 08:00-22:00 (thresholds are fictional operator report thresholds)
Date        RSRP samples  SS-RSRP<-115dBm  SS-SINR<0dB  RRC.ConnMean  RRU.PrbDl(%)
2026-11-17  51,800         8%               9%          112           44
2026-11-18  52,000         8%              10%          110           45
2026-11-19  52,300         9%               9%          113           44
2026-11-20  49,100        28%              27%           71           29
2026-11-21  49,500        29%              28%           69           28

Neighbours, same period:   RSRP and SINR shares within ±1 point of their own baseline
Change calendar (operator):  no planned work recorded for site KH-0412 on 11-19/11-20
New construction:           none reported in the sector
```

**011.** Which interpretation fits the data best?

1. A capacity problem started on 11-20, because RRC.ConnMean and RRU.PrbDl changed on that day
2. A cell-level downlink change is indicated; both low-RSRP and low-SINR shares jumped only on KH-0412-2.
3. Overlapping coverage appeared, because the SINR share rose
4. A new building shadowed the sector, because coverage problems follow location

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　2

**詳解**　A one-day step in both the low-RSRP and low-SINR shares, limited to one cell, with fewer connected users and lower PRB load (users served elsewhere), points to a cell-level change in what the cell transmits. Option 1 is wrong because load fell, which is the consequence of shrinking coverage, not congestion. Option 3 is wrong because overlapping coverage shows normal RSRP with low SINR, whereas RSRP fell here. Option 4 is wrong because no construction is reported and a building would not appear overnight across the whole cell.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.215 V18.4.0 clauses 5.1.1 and 5.1.5 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.215/); 3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.2.1 and 5.1.1.4.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/); Curriculum 6.1.1

</details>

**012.** Which typical causes of coverage holes does TS 37.320 Annex A list?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Physical obstructions such as new buildings or hills, unsuitable antenna parameters, or inadequate RF planning.

**詳解**　Annex A states that coverage holes are usually caused by physical obstructions such as new buildings, hills, or by unsuitable antenna parameters, or just inadequate RF planning, and that UEs in a coverage hole suffer call drop and radio link failure. In this scenario no new building is reported, which keeps "unsuitable antenna parameters" (or a hardware fault) on the list.

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 Annex A (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/)

</details>

**013.** An engineer wants to add the idle-mode SS-SINR of UEs in this sector to the analysis. According to TS 38.215, in which RRC states is SS-SINR (when not used for L1-SINR) applicable?

1. RRC_IDLE and RRC_INACTIVE, intra-frequency and inter-frequency
2. RRC_IDLE, RRC_INACTIVE and RRC_CONNECTED, intra-frequency only
3. RRC_CONNECTED intra-frequency and RRC_CONNECTED inter-frequency only
4. All RRC states, because SINR is measured on the SSS like SS-RSRP

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　3

**詳解**　TS 38.215 clause 5.1.5 lists SS-SINR (otherwise than for L1-SINR) as applicable for RRC_CONNECTED intra-frequency and RRC_CONNECTED inter-frequency, so idle-mode SINR is not available. Option 1 lists idle and inactive states, which belong to SS-RSRP's applicability, and omits RRC_CONNECTED. Option 2 wrongly adds idle and inactive states. Option 4 confuses SS-SINR with SS-RSRP, which (otherwise than for L1-RSRP) is applicable in RRC_IDLE, RRC_INACTIVE and RRC_CONNECTED.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.215 V18.4.0 clauses 5.1.1 and 5.1.5 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.215/)

</details>

**014.** The shift supervisor wants to write "RU hardware fault on KH-0412-2" in the ticket. What does the data support?

1. Cannot identify a hardware fault without alarm and configuration history.
2. A hardware fault is confirmed, because no planned work is recorded in the change calendar
3. A hardware fault is excluded, because hardware faults always raise alarms that would have closed the ticket automatically
4. A parameter change is confirmed, because the change happened overnight

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　1

**詳解**　The data show a cell-level downlink change but nothing about its cause. A power or tilt change by an operator or by a SON function, and an RF hardware fault, all produce the same RSRP shift; the alarm list (6.1.7) and configuration history including notifyMOIAttributeValueChanges (6.1.6, SON) are needed. Option 2 is wrong because an empty change calendar does not exclude unplanned or SON changes. Option 3 is an invented rule. Option 4 is wrong because timing alone does not identify the cause.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.532 V18.8.0 clause 11.1.1.9 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.532/); Curriculum 6.1.1 (worked example, "conclusions that cannot be drawn")

</details>

**015.** How many more samples below -115 dBm were recorded on 2026-11-21 than on 2026-11-18?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　10,195 more samples.

**詳解**　2026-11-18: 52,000 × 8% = 4,160 samples. 2026-11-21: 49,500 × 29% = 14,355 samples. Difference: 14,355 − 4,160 = 10,195. The absolute count rose by more than three times even though the total sample count fell by 2,500.

**出處**　Curriculum 6.1.1; 3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.22.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

#### Timing advance as a distance indicator for overshoot

**情境 005**　Co-sited cells on two different subcarrier spacings

At rural site RL-0905 (fictional), sector 1 carries an n78 cell and an n28 cell. A junior engineer wrote the note below after reading the vendor's TA percentile report.

```
Site RL-0905, sector 1, week 2026-11-16 .. 11-22
Cell         Band  SCS     Planned radius  TA index p50  TA index p95
RL-0905-1A   n78   30 kHz  1,500 m         18            41
RL-0905-1B   n28   15 kHz  4,000 m         20            61
(planned radii are fictional planning values)

Engineer note (draft):
 "1A p95 = 41 x 39.06 m = 1,602 m -> about 100 m beyond plan, ignore.
  1B p95 = 61 x 39.06 m = 2,383 m -> well inside the 4 km plan, no overshoot.
  Conclusion: sector 1 has no overshoot on either layer."
```

**021.** What is the correct distance for the p95 TA index of RL-0905-1B?

1. About 4,766 m
2. About 2,383 m
3. About 9,531 m
4. About 1,191 m

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　1

**詳解**　At SCS 15 kHz (μ = 0) one step is 16·64 = 1,024 Tc ≈ 5.2083×10^-7 s round trip, so 3×10^8 × 5.2083×10^-7 / 2 ≈ 78.125 m. 61 × 78.125 m ≈ 4,766 m. Option 2 is the engineer's figure using the 30 kHz step (61 × 39.0625). Option 3 forgets to halve the round-trip time. Option 4 uses a 60 kHz step (61 × 19.53).

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.213 V18.9.0 clause 4.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.213/); 3GPP TS 38.211 V18.10.0 clause 4.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.211/)

</details>

**022.** After correcting the calculation, which statement best replaces the engineer's conclusion?

1. Both layers are inside plan, so the conclusion stands
2. 1B is ~766 m beyond plan; 1A is ~102 m over, weak evidence by itself.
3. RL-0905-1A is the overshooting cell, because its p95 is beyond plan while 1B's is not
4. Both layers are overshooting by the same amount, because they share the same antenna direction

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　2

**詳解**　1B: 4,765.6 − 4,000 = 765.6 m beyond plan. 1A: 41 × 39.0625 = 1,601.6 m, i.e. 101.6 m beyond 1,500 m, only about 2.6 TA steps, and TA uses the first detected path, so a small excess is weak evidence. Option 1 keeps the wrong 1B value. Option 3 reverses the finding. Option 4 is unsupported: the two layers have different bands, planned radii and excesses.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.213 V18.9.0 clause 4.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.213/); 3GPP TS 38.215 V18.4.0 clause 5.2.7 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.215/)

</details>

**023.** State in one sentence what the calculation error in the engineer's note is.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　The note applies the 30 kHz step (≈39.06 m) to the 15 kHz cell RL-0905-1B, whose step is ≈78.13 m, so the computed distance is only half the true value.

**詳解**　NTA per index is 16·64/2^μ Tc, so the step in metres depends on the SCS: 39.0625 m at μ = 1 and 78.125 m at μ = 0. 61 × 78.125 ≈ 4,766 m instead of 2,383 m.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.213 V18.9.0 clause 4.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.213/)

</details>

**024.** Can you conclude from the percentile report that RL-0905-1B forms an "island" of coverage inside another cell's area?

1. Yes, because the p95 is beyond the planned radius
2. Yes, because an n28 cell always reaches farther than an n78 cell
3. No; TA gives distance, not which other cell covers those users.
4. No, because an island can only occur on cells with SCS 30 kHz

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　3

**詳解**　TS 37.320 Annex A describes overshoot as coverage far beyond the plan that can form an island inside another cell, which may not be a direct neighbour. Showing an island requires knowing which other cells' areas the far UEs are in, e.g. from neighbour measurements or handover statistics per cell pair. Option 1 confuses distance with location inside another cell. Option 2 is a generalisation, not evidence for this cell. Option 4 is invented.

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 Annex A (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/); 3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.22.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**025.** Why does the distance represented by one TA index step halve when the subcarrier spacing doubles in NR?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Because NTA per index is 16·64/2^μ Tc, so each doubling of the SCS (μ + 1) halves the time, and therefore the distance, represented by one step.

**詳解**　TS 38.213 clause 4.2 gives NTA = TA·16·64/2^μ. With Tc fixed by TS 38.211, μ = 0, 1, 2 give about 78.13 m, 39.06 m and 19.53 m per step (round trip halved, c ≈ 3×10^8 m/s).

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.213 V18.9.0 clause 4.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.213/); 3GPP TS 38.211 V18.10.0 clause 4.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.211/)

</details>

**情境 006**　TA percentiles compared with inter-site distance

NorthLink Mobile (fictional) screens urban cells of site UR-0101 (all SCS 30 kHz). The planned radius and the screening rule are fictional operator rules.

| Cell | First-tier site in main-lobe direction | Inter-site distance (m) | Planned radius = 0.6 × ISD (m) | TA index p90 | Terrain in main lobe |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UR-0101-1 | UR-0102 | 650 | 390 | 14 | crosses the river |
| UR-0101-2 | UR-0109 | 1,100 | 660 | 15 | dense residential |
| UR-0101-3 | UR-0117 | 900 | 540 | 22 | flat industrial zone |

Screening rule (fictional operator rule): flag a cell for overshoot review when the TA-derived p90 distance exceeds 1.3 × planned radius.

**026.** Which cells are flagged by the screening rule?

1. UR-0101-3 only
2. UR-0101-1 and UR-0101-2
3. All three cells
4. UR-0101-1 and UR-0101-3

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　4

**詳解**　UR-0101-1: 14 × 39.0625 = 546.9 m; 546.9 / 390 ≈ 1.40 > 1.3, flagged. UR-0101-2: 15 × 39.0625 = 585.9 m; 585.9 / 660 ≈ 0.89, not flagged. UR-0101-3: 22 × 39.0625 = 859.4 m; 859.4 / 540 ≈ 1.59 > 1.3, flagged. Option 1 misses UR-0101-1 (the smallest absolute distance, but the smallest ISD too). Option 2 includes UR-0101-2, whose p90 is inside its radius. Option 3 includes UR-0101-2 as well.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.213 V18.9.0 clause 4.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.213/); screening rule is a fictional operator rule; Curriculum 6.1.1

</details>

**027.** What is the TA-derived p90 distance of UR-0101-3 and its ratio to the planned radius?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　About 859 m, i.e. about 1.59 × the 540 m planned radius.

**詳解**　22 × 39.0625 m = 859.375 m. Planned radius 0.6 × 900 m = 540 m. 859.375 / 540 ≈ 1.59, above the 1.3 screening limit.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.213 V18.9.0 clause 4.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.213/); screening rule is a fictional operator rule

</details>

**028.** Which reasons for overshoot does TS 37.320 Annex A explicitly mention?

1. Missing neighbour relations and wrong PCI planning
2. Reflections in buildings or across open water, lakes etc.
3. High PRB load in the neighbouring cells
4. UE maximum transmit power limitations

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　2

**詳解**　Annex A states that reasons for overshoot may be reflections in buildings or across open water, lakes etc., and that overshoot may appear as an island in the interior of another cell. Option 1 describes neighbour/PCI problems (6.1.3, 6.1.6), which can coexist but are not listed as overshoot reasons. Option 3 is a capacity matter. Option 4 relates to UL coverage (6.1.10).

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 Annex A (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/)

</details>

**029.** Which possible actions against overshoot does TS 37.320 Annex A list?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Changing the coverage of certain cells, and mobility by exclude-listing of certain cells.

**詳解**　Annex A says UEs in an overshoot area may suffer call drops or high interference, and that possible actions include changing the coverage of certain cells and mobility by exclude-listing of certain cells. In operations both are parameter changes that need approval (6.3.2).

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 Annex A (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/)

</details>

**030.** For UR-0101-1, whose main lobe crosses the river, can the team already confirm that the cell overshoots and harms users on the other bank?

1. No; reflections can inflate TA, and the data do not show user harm.
2. Yes, because the cell is flagged by the screening rule
3. Yes, because water always causes overshoot
4. No, because a cell can only overshoot if its TA p90 exceeds 2 km

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　1

**詳解**　The rule only flags candidates. TADV is based on the first detected PRACH path, and Annex A mentions that overshoot may come from reflections across water, so the distance needs confirmation, and harm (call drops, high interference) needs its own evidence. Option 2 treats a screening flag as a diagnosis. Option 3 overgeneralises Annex A ("may"). Option 4 is an invented absolute threshold.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.215 V18.4.0 clause 5.2.7 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.215/); 3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 Annex A (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/)

</details>

#### Overshoot seen through handover and neighbour-cell data

**情境 007**　Handover executions per cell pair from one source cell

Telco-X (fictional) reviews outgoing inter-gNB handovers of cell BR-0330-1 for one week. Distances are from the source site to the target site.

```
Source NRCellCU: BR-0330-1      Period: 2026-11-17 .. 2026-11-23
Counters per NRCellRelation (TS 28.552 names)

Target cell  Site distance  Tier (planning)          MM.HoExeInterReq  MM.HoExeInterSucc  MM.HoExeInterFail.RrcReestabReq
BR-0331-2    0.7 km         first tier               4,820             4,771              31
BR-0336-1    0.9 km         first tier               3,150             3,108              25
BR-0342-3    1.1 km         first tier               2,210             2,176              20
RV-0815-1    4.2 km         third tier, across river   640               518              97

(other failure subcounters not shown)
```

**031.** Which observation in the table points most directly to an overshoot situation?

1. BR-0331-2 receives the largest number of handover requests
2. All first-tier targets have handover success rates between about 98.5% and 99.0%
3. The 4.2 km third-tier cell receives 640 requests at lower success than first-tier targets.
4. MM.HoExeInterFail.RrcReestabReq is non-zero for every target cell

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　3

**詳解**　Handovers between cells that are not direct neighbours, especially across open water, mean that the two cells' coverage meets where the plan says it should not, which is the overshoot pattern described in TS 37.320 Annex A (island of coverage, possibly not a direct neighbour, reflections across water). Option 1 is normal: the closest first-tier cell usually receives the most handovers. Option 2 describes healthy first-tier relations. Option 4 is normal: a few re-establishments after handover failure occur on every relation.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.6.1.7 to 5.1.1.6.1.9 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/); 3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 Annex A (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/)

</details>

**032.** What is the handover execution success rate from BR-0330-1 to RV-0815-1?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　About 80.9%.

**詳解**　MM.HoExeInterSucc / MM.HoExeInterReq = 518 / 640 ≈ 0.8094, i.e. 80.9%. For comparison the first-tier relations are 4,771 / 4,820 ≈ 98.98%, 3,108 / 3,150 ≈ 98.67% and 2,176 / 2,210 ≈ 98.46%.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.6.1.7 and 5.1.1.6.1.8 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**033.** A colleague says: "Handovers to a far cell are just a neighbour-list problem; once the relation is cleaned up the overshoot is solved." Which statement is correct?

1. Overshoot and neighbour-relation faults are distinct and can coexist; check both.
2. Overshoot is by definition a missing neighbour relation, so 6.1.3 is the only place to handle it
3. Overshoot cannot cause handover failures, so failures to a far cell always mean a neighbour-relation error
4. A neighbour relation to a far cell proves the far cell is not overshooting, because the relation was planned

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　1

**詳解**　Overshoot often makes UEs hand over to distant cells or fail handover, which looks like a 6.1.3 neighbour problem, but the cause is coverage beyond the plan, whereas a neighbour problem is a relation setting; both need checking and may coexist. Option 2 merges the two causes. Option 3 contradicts TS 37.320 Annex A, which says UEs in an overshoot area may suffer call drops or high interference. Option 4 is wrong because an existing relation (possibly created automatically) says nothing about whether the coverage is as planned.

**出處**　Curriculum 6.1.1 ("easily confused points"); 3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 Annex A (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/)

</details>

**034.** On which measured objects are MM.HoExeInterReq and MM.HoExeInterSucc defined in TS 28.552, which is what makes a per-cell-pair table like this possible?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　NRCellCU and NRCellRelation.

**詳解**　TS 28.552 clauses 5.1.1.6.1.7 and 5.1.1.6.1.8 list the measured object as "NRCellCU; NRCellRelation", so the counters can be read per source cell and per relation (cell pair). The requested-execution counter is stepped when the source sends the RRCReconfiguration denoting a legacy handover.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.6.1.7 and 5.1.1.6.1.8 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**035.** Which cell is overshooting: BR-0330-1 (its coverage reaches towards the river) or RV-0815-1 (its coverage reaches into BR-0330-1's area)?

1. RV-0815-1, because it is the target of the handovers
2. Cannot tell from counters alone; UE location at handover is needed.
3. BR-0330-1, because it is the source cell of the handovers
4. Both, because handovers always happen at the midpoint between two sites

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　2

**詳解**　Handovers BR-0330-1 → RV-0815-1 only show that UEs served by BR-0330-1 measured RV-0815-1 strongly. That happens either when BR-0330-1 serves UEs far away near the river (BR-0330-1 overshooting) or when RV-0815-1's signal reaches BR-0330-1's own area (RV-0815-1 overshooting). The TA of the handing-over UEs or their locations would separate the two. Options 1 and 3 infer direction from the handover direction, which is not valid. Option 4 is an invented rule.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.6.1.7 and 5.1.1.33.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/); 3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 clause 5.4.1.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/)

</details>

**情境 008**　Unknown strong PCI resolved to a hill-top cell

NorthLink Mobile (fictional) serving cell HB-0118-2 (PCI 412) received measurement reports containing an unknown PCI; the automatic neighbour relation function resolved it via a CGI report.

```
Serving cell HB-0118-2 (PCI 412), 2026-11-24, measurement reports with unknown PCI 731
time      UE      serving SS-RSRP (dBm)   PCI 731 SS-RSRP (dBm)
09:02:11  UE-a    -99                     -93
09:05:40  UE-b    -101                    -95
09:11:03  UE-c    -97                     -96
09:17:28  UE-d    -100                    -94
09:20:55  UE-e    -98                     -97
09:26:13  UE-f    -102                    -92
09:31:47  UE-g    -96                     -98
09:40:02  UE-h    -99                     -95

CGI report 09:02:30: PCI 731 -> cell KM-0960-1
KM-0960-1: hill-top site, antenna height 62 m, 7.5 km from HB-0118, not in planning neighbour set
NRCellRelation HB-0118-2 -> KM-0960-1: did not exist before 09:02; ANR proposal pending approval
First-tier neighbours of HB-0118-2: HB-0119-1 (PCI 415), HB-0124-3 (PCI 88)
```

**036.** What is the most consistent interpretation of these reports?

1. PCI 731 is a PCI confusion between two first-tier neighbours, so the CGI report should be ignored
2. HB-0118-2 is overshooting, because it is the serving cell in all reports
3. The reports show a capacity problem in HB-0118-2, because many UEs report a second cell
4. KM-0960-1 is measured at comparable or higher RSRP 7.5 km away, consistent with overshoot.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　4

**詳解**　UEs located in HB-0118-2's area (they are served there) measure the far hill-top cell at similar or higher RSRP than the serving cell; a high antenna far outside the planned neighbour set is a typical overshoot source. Option 1 is wrong because the CGI report identified a single specific cell that is not a first-tier neighbour (the first-tier PCIs are 415 and 88). Option 2 reverses the direction: the far signal arrives in HB-0118-2's area. Option 3 is wrong because the reports show radio levels, not load.

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 Annex A (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/); Curriculum 6.1.1

</details>

**037.** What is the most appropriate next step before deciding on the ANR proposal?

1. Approve the ANR proposal and close the ticket, because a relation removes the problem
2. Change the PCI of HB-0118-2 to avoid PCI 731
3. Check KM-0960-1 tilt/power history and TA distribution before approving ANR.
4. Increase the transmit power of HB-0118-2 so that it dominates again

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　3

**詳解**　Whether the far cell's coverage changed or was always too large decides the fix (coverage change versus relation); configuration history and the far cell's L1M.ATADist.Bin are read-only checks to do first. Option 1 treats the relation as the cure; adding a relation to an overshooting cell can even attract more handovers to it. Option 2 addresses a PCI problem that the data do not show. Option 4 is a service-affecting change that increases interference and should not precede diagnosis.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.313 V18.2.0 Table 7.2.3.2.1-1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.313/); 3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.33.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/); Curriculum 6.1.1 and 6.3.1

</details>

**038.** Does the signal of KM-0960-1 (PCI 731) already cause dropped connections in HB-0118-2? Answer from the data and say what is missing.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Cannot be determined: the log only contains measurement reports; RLF reports, handover failure counters or drop statistics for HB-0118-2 during the same period are needed.

**詳解**　TS 37.320 Annex A says UEs in an overshoot area may suffer call drops or high interference, but "may" is not evidence. The data show signal levels only. RLF reports (with latest serving and neighbour measurements) or MM.HoExeInterFail and drop counters would show whether users are affected.

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 clause 5.4.1.2 and Annex A (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/)

</details>

**039.** Once the relation exists, which TS 28.552 measurement gives the per-SSB distribution of KM-0960-1's SS-RSRP as reported by HB-0118-2's UEs, and on which object is it collected?

1. L1M.SS-RSRPNrNbr.SSBIndex.Bin, per NRCellRelation, from RRC reports.
2. L1M.SS-RSRP.Bin, collected per Beam of HB-0118-2
3. MR.NRScSSSINR.BinX, collected per NRCellCU
4. L1M.RSRPEutraNbr.Bin, collected per EUtranCellRelation

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　1

**詳解**　TS 28.552 clause 5.1.1.22.2 defines L1M.SS-RSRPNrNbr.SSBIndex.Bin as the SS-RSRP per SSB of a neighbour NR cell, incremented when a UE reports it via MeasurementReport, with object NRCellRelation; before 09:02 there was no relation, so no such counter existed for this pair. Option 2 is the serving cell's own L1-RSRP distribution. Option 3 is the serving cell's SINR distribution. Option 4 is for E-UTRA neighbours, not NR.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.22.1, 5.1.1.22.2, 5.1.1.22.3 and 5.1.1.32.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**040.** In how many of the eight reports was PCI 731 more than 3 dB stronger than the serving cell? (The 3 dB margin is a fictional operator analysis threshold.)

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　5 reports (UE-a, UE-b, UE-d, UE-f, UE-h).

**詳解**　Differences (PCI 731 minus serving): UE-a −93 − (−99) = +6; UE-b +6; UE-c +1; UE-d +6; UE-e +1; UE-f +10; UE-g −2; UE-h +4. Values above +3 dB: +6, +6, +6, +10, +4, i.e. five reports.

**出處**　Curriculum 6.1.1; 3GPP TS 38.215 V18.4.0 clause 5.1.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.215/)

</details>

**情境 009**　Apparent overshoot during a neighbour outage

Telco-X (fictional) overshoot screening flagged cell KH-0412-3 on 2026-11-22. The night-shift log and hourly counters are below (SCS 30 kHz).

```
2026-11-22  timeline (site KH-0415 is first-tier to KH-0412 in the east)
02:10  alarm raised  KH-0415-1  "cell out of service" (vendor-specific text)
11:40  alarm cleared KH-0415-1

Cell KH-0412-3 hourly counters
hour   TA index p90   MM.HoExeInterReq -> KH-0419-2   SS-RSRP<-115dBm share
01:00  16              3                              7%
02:00  17              4                              8%
03:00  31             48                             17%
06:00  33             52                             19%
10:00  31             61                             18%
12:00  17              5                              8%
13:00  16              3                              7%

KH-0419-2: second-tier cell, 2.3 km east of KH-0412, beyond KH-0415
Screening output: "KH-0412-3 flagged: TA p90 > 1.3 x planned radius (03:00-10:00)"
```

**041.** What is the best explanation for the flag?

1. KH-0412-3's downtilt was reduced at 03:00, so it overshoots and should be downtilted again
2. The outage shifted traffic and indicators temporarily; this does not establish overshoot.
3. KH-0419-2 started overshooting at 03:00 and pulled UEs away from KH-0412-3
4. The screening tool has a bug, because TA cannot change within one hour

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　2

**詳解**　All three indicators change within the alarm window (02:10–11:40) and return to normal by 12:00. When a first-tier neighbour is down, the remaining cells pick up its UEs at larger distances and weaker levels, and some hand over to the next cell beyond. Option 1 ignores the alarm and the return to normal without any change. Option 3 is wrong because handovers to KH-0419-2 rose, consistent with UEs east of the dead cell, and nothing shows KH-0419-2 changed. Option 4 is wrong because TA follows where the served UEs are, which changed when the neighbour went down.

**出處**　Curriculum 6.1.1 (coverage follows location; check alarms before concluding); 3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.6.1.7 and 5.1.1.33.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**042.** What distance does KH-0412-3's TA p90 at 03:00 correspond to?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　About 1,211 m.

**詳解**　At SCS 30 kHz one TA step ≈ 39.0625 m (512 Tc round trip, halved, c ≈ 3×10^8 m/s). 31 × 39.0625 m = 1,210.9 m, compared with 17 × 39.0625 = 664.1 m at 02:00.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.213 V18.9.0 clause 4.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.213/)

</details>

**043.** Which TS 37.320 Annex A coverage use case is about knowing where cell boundaries actually are, in order to compare them with the planned network, noting that poor handover performance may result from changed boundaries (e.g. new buildings, a bridge or a tunnel)?

1. Coverage mapping
2. Weak coverage
3. Cell boundary mapping
4. Coverage mapping for pico cell in CA scenario

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　3

**詳解**　Annex A describes "cell boundary mapping" as knowing the location of intra/inter-RAT cell boundaries to compare to the expected/planned setting, and notes that poor handover performance may be caused by changed boundaries due to new buildings, a bridge or a tunnel. Option 1 is about signal levels in all parts of the network. Option 2 is about the serving cell being below a planned performance requirement. Option 4 is about where a pico cell can be added as an SCell.

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 Annex A (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/)

</details>

**044.** According to TS 37.320 Annex A, where should measurements for "coverage mapping" be collected?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　In all parts of the network, not just in the areas where there are potential coverage issues.

**詳解**　Annex A states that coverage mapping needs knowledge of signal levels across the cell areas to get a complete view, which means measurements collected everywhere. A complete map is what allows a time-limited distortion such as this outage to be recognised against the normal picture.

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 Annex A (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/)

</details>

**045.** Did the outage leave some users in KH-0415-1's area without any usable coverage between 02:10 and 11:40?

1. No, because KH-0412-3 took over the traffic
2. Yes, because KH-0412-3's low-RSRP share more than doubled
3. No, because the alarm was cleared at 11:40
4. Cannot determine from serving-cell data alone.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　4

**詳解**　UEs that found no suitable cell do not appear in any serving cell's counters. RLF reports (including the "no suitable cell is found" flag when T311 expires) and Logged MDT any-cell-selection records are the evidence for a hole. Option 1 assumes full takeover, which the data cannot show. Option 2 is wrong because a higher low-RSRP share among served UEs is not proof of unserved UEs. Option 3 confuses the end of the outage with what happened during it.

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 clauses 5.1.1.2 and 5.4.1.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/)

</details>

#### Overlapping coverage (pilot pollution) and the number of comparable cells

**情境 011**　Before and after a new small site in a dense area

NorthLink Mobile (fictional) put site DT-0533 on air in a pedestrian zone. The table compares the two weeks before and after for the surrounding cells (fictional report thresholds).

```
Change record CR-25-1187: 2026-11-12  DT-0533 (3 sectors) on air
  note: "configuredMaxTxPower set to the same value as the surrounding macro cells"
  distance DT-0533 to DT-0507: 180 m, to DT-0511: 210 m, to DT-0520: 650 m

Busy hour, pre = 10-29 .. 11-11, post = 11-13 .. 11-26
Cell        median nbrs within 6 dB   SS-SINR<0dB share   SS-RSRP<-115dBm share   DRB.UEThpDl (Mbit/s)
            pre / post                pre / post          pre / post              pre / post
DT-0507-1   1 / 3                     8% / 26%            2% / 1%                 88 / 51
DT-0507-2   1 / 1                     7% / 8%             3% / 3%                 92 / 90
DT-0511-3   2 / 4                     11% / 34%           1% / 1%                 74 / 39
DT-0520-1   0 / 1                     5% / 9%             6% / 2%                 97 / 95
```

**051.** Which statement matches the table?

1. All four cells were degraded by the new site in the same way
2. DT-0520-1 is the most degraded cell, because its number of comparable neighbours increased
3. DT-0507-1 and DT-0511-3 show overlap: SINR worsened while RSRP stayed good; DT-0520-1 improved.
4. DT-0507-2 shows weak coverage after the change, because its RSRP share stayed at 3%

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　3

**詳解**　DT-0507-1 (+2 comparable cells, SINR<0 share +18 points) and DT-0511-3 (+2, +23 points) degraded while RSRP stayed good; DT-0520-1, 650 m away, gained coverage (low-RSRP share 6% → 2%) with only a small SINR change. Option 1 ignores DT-0507-2 and DT-0520-1, which barely changed. Option 2 misreads a 0 → 1 change with a 4-point SINR change as the worst. Option 4 is wrong because a constant 3% low-RSRP share is no change.

**出處**　Curriculum 6.1.1 (characteristics table); 3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.3.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**052.** Was DT-0533's configuredMaxTxPower set according to its radio plan?

1. Yes, because the change record says it equals the surrounding macro cells
2. No, because a small site must always use lower power than a macro site
3. Yes, because the change record was approved
4. Cannot determine; no planned DT-0533 power value is given.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　4

**詳解**　"Same as the surrounding macro cells" describes what was configured, not what was planned. A configuration-versus-plan comparison (6.1.6) needs the planned value. Option 1 confuses the configured value with the plan. Option 2 is an invented "always" rule. Option 3 is wrong because approval of a change does not prove its values match the radio plan.

**出處**　Curriculum 6.1.1 and 6.1.6; 3GPP TS 28.313 V18.2.0 Table 7.2.3.2.1-1 (configuredMaxTxPower) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.313/)

</details>

**053.** Write the recommendation entry for a ticket on the cell changes seen around DT-0507, DT-0511 and DT-0520 after DT-0533 went on air.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Required points:
- DT-0507-1 and DT-0511-3 show overlapping coverage after DT-0533 went on air (more cells within 6 dB, low-SINR share up 18 and 23 points, RSRP unchanged).
- DT-0520-1 benefited (low-RSRP share 6% → 2%), so DT-0533 should not simply be switched off.
- Confirm which DT-0533 sectors are within 6 dB in DT-0507-1/DT-0511-3 and compare DT-0533's configured power and tilt with its plan.
- Proposed fix: reduce DT-0533's coverage (e.g. power or tilt) as an approved change, then re-measure.

**詳解**　The table supports the overlap diagnosis for two cells and a coverage gain for one, so the recommendation must keep the gain while reducing overlap. Reducing cell coverage is the remedy TS 37.320 Annex A gives for pilot pollution; any power/tilt change is service-affecting and needs approval (6.3.2).

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 Annex A (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/); Curriculum 6.1.1 and 6.3.2

</details>

**054.** Which of the following are listed in TS 28.313 Table 7.2.3.2.1-1 as CCO control parameters that the C-SON function may update?

1. All five listed CCO control parameters.
2. a3-Offset, hysteresis and timeToTrigger
3. nRPCI, nRTAC and administrativeState
4. RRU.PrbDl and DRB.UEThpDl

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　1

**詳解**　Table 7.2.3.2.1-1 lists configured max Tx power, configured max Tx EIRP, coverage shape, digital tilt and digital azimuth, each referring to the TS 28.541 attribute. Option 2 lists TS 38.331 handover parameters, not CCO parameters. Option 3 lists identity and state attributes. Option 4 lists performance measurements, which CCO consumes as input (Table 7.2.3.3.1-1), not parameters it changes.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.313 V18.2.0 Tables 7.2.3.2.1-1 and 7.2.3.3.1-1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.313/)

</details>

**055.** By what percentage did DRB.UEThpDl of DT-0511-3 fall from the pre to the post period?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　About 47.3%.

**詳解**　(74 − 39) / 74 = 35 / 74 ≈ 0.473, i.e. a drop of about 47.3%. For DT-0507-1 the drop is (88 − 51) / 88 ≈ 42.0%.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.3.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**情境 012**　Immediate MDT records at different heights of a tower block

Telco-X (fictional) collected Immediate MDT M1 records with GNSS altitude inside a 26-storey residential tower after complaints about "full bars but slow data" on the upper floors.

```
Immediate MDT M1, 2026-11-23 19:00-21:00, tower block near site TW-0201
rec  altitude  serving cell / SS-RSRP     other cells within 6 dB of serving (SS-RSRP)            SS-SINR
R1    4 m      TW-0201-1 / -113 dBm       none (best other: TW-0207-2 -122)                      1 dB
R2    9 m      TW-0201-1 / -110 dBm       none (best other: TW-0207-2 -119)                      3 dB
R6   30 m      TW-0201-1 / -95 dBm        none (best other: TW-0207-2 -103)                      9 dB
R3   45 m      TW-0201-1 / -86 dBm        TW-0207-2 -87, TW-0198-3 -89, TW-0215-1 -90            -3 dB
R4   61 m      TW-0207-2 / -83 dBm        TW-0201-1 -84, TW-0198-3 -85, TW-0215-1 -86, TW-0230-2 -88   -5 dB
R5   78 m      TW-0198-3 / -82 dBm        TW-0207-2 -83, TW-0201-1 -85, TW-0230-2 -86            -4 dB
```

**056.** Which records show the overlapping-coverage signature?

1. R1 and R2
2. R3, R4 and R5
3. R6 only
4. All six records

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　2

**詳解**　R3–R5 have strong serving RSRP (-82 to -86 dBm), three or four other cells within 6 dB and negative SINR: no dominant cell. Option 1 picks the low-floor records, which have weak RSRP and no comparable cells (weak coverage). Option 3 picks a record with one clearly dominant cell and good SINR. Option 4 mixes all three situations.

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 clause 5.4.1.1 and Annex A (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/); Curriculum 6.1.1

</details>

**057.** How many other cells are within 6 dB of the serving cell in record R4?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　4.

**詳解**　Serving TW-0207-2 at -83 dBm; differences: TW-0201-1 1 dB, TW-0198-3 2 dB, TW-0215-1 3 dB, TW-0230-2 5 dB. All four are within 6 dB.

**出處**　Curriculum 6.1.1; 3GPP TS 38.215 V18.4.0 clause 5.1.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.215/)

</details>

**058.** Which measurement collection triggers does TS 37.320 define for Immediate MDT M1 in NR?

1. End of measurement collection period only
2. Reception of a power headroom report according to the existing RRM configuration
3. Existing-RRM events or MDT-configured periodic/A2-triggered collection.
4. The L1 measurement-quantity event and the out-of-coverage detection trigger

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　3

**詳解**　TS 37.320 clause 5.4.1.1 lists for M1: event-triggered measurement reports according to existing RRM configuration for events A1, A2, A3, A4, A5, A6, B1 or B2, and periodic, A2 event-triggered, or A2 event-triggered periodic reports according to MDT-specific configuration. Option 1 is the trigger for M4–M7. Option 2 is the trigger for M2. Option 4 lists the event-based triggers of Logged MDT (clause 5.1.1.1.1), not Immediate MDT.

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 clauses 5.1.1.1.1 and 5.4.1.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/)

</details>

**059.** According to TS 37.320, how are Immediate MDT M1 measurements tagged with location?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　The UE includes detailed location information (e.g. GNSS location information) if it is available in the UE when the measurement was taken.

**詳解**　Clause 5.4.1.4 states that M1 measurements are tagged by the UE with detailed location information if available, reported as specified in TS 38.331. Availability depends on the UE, so some records may have no location or no altitude.

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 clause 5.4.1.4 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/)

</details>

**060.** Do the records prove that the upper-floor residents get lower user throughput than the lower-floor residents?

1. Yes, because SINR is negative on the upper floors
2. No, because RSRP is better on the upper floors, so throughput must be higher there
3. Yes, because the residents complained about slow data
4. Cannot determine; same-floor, same-time UE throughput is missing.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　4

**詳解**　M1 carries DL signal quantities only. Throughput also depends on load and scheduling, so it must be measured; TS 37.320 clause 5.4.1.1 defines M5 (average UE throughput per DRB per UE, by gNB). Option 1 infers throughput from SINR alone. Option 2 infers it from RSRP alone, ignoring the negative SINR. Option 3 treats the complaint as measurement.

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 clause 5.4.1.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/)

</details>

#### MDT and RLF reports as evidence of coverage holes

**情境 013**　Logged MDT records around a new shopping mall

Telco-X (fictional) ran a management-based Logged MDT session in the cells around a shopping mall that opened on 2026-11-10 in grid square G7. The decoded records retrieved on 2026-11-24 are below (grid squares are the operator's own location grid).

```
Trace session TRS-0447, Logged MDT, cells NP-0610-1 / NP-0610-2, 2026-11-23
UE      time       logged content                                   location
UE-03   18:42:10   camped NP-0610-2, RSRP -112 dBm, RSRQ -16 dB     G7
UE-03   18:42:41   anyCellSelectionDetected                         G7
UE-08   19:05:02   anyCellSelectionDetected                         G7
UE-11   19:20:33   camped NP-0610-2, RSRP -114 dBm, RSRQ -17 dB     G8
UE-11   19:20:59   anyCellSelectionDetected                         G7
UE-17   19:31:18   anyCellSelectionDetected                         (no location)
UE-22   20:02:47   anyCellSelectionDetected                         (no location)
UE-25   20:15:12   camped NP-0610-1, RSRP -97 dBm, RSRQ -11 dB      G2
UE-29   20:40:05   anyCellSelectionDetected                         G7

G7: block containing the new shopping mall; G8: street east of the mall; G2: park 600 m west
```

**061.** How many different UEs logged anyCellSelectionDetected with a location in grid square G7?

1. 6
2. 4
3. 3
4. 5

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　2

**詳解**　G7 entries with anyCellSelectionDetected: UE-03 (18:42:41), UE-08, UE-11 (19:20:59, logged in G7 although its previous sample was in G8) and UE-29: four UEs. Option 1 also counts UE-17 and UE-22, which have no location. Option 3 misses UE-11 because its camped sample was in G8. Option 4 adds one of the UEs without location.

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 clause 5.1.1.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/)

</details>

**062.** Which downlink quantities does an NR UE log for the camped cell in Logged MDT downlink pilot strength logging?

1. RSRP, RSRQ, best SSB beam, and good-beam count.
2. RSRP, RSRQ and SINR
3. RSRP only, because RSRQ is an LTE quantity
4. SINR and power headroom

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　1

**詳解**　TS 37.320 clause 5.1.1.2 says the logged quantities are fixed and consist of both RSRP and RSRQ for NR, and that for NR the best beam index (SSB index) with the best beam RSRP/RSRQ and the number of good beams are logged as well. Option 2 adds SINR, which is not a Logged MDT quantity (SS-SINR is only applicable in RRC_CONNECTED per TS 38.215). Option 3 wrongly drops RSRQ. Option 4 mixes in power headroom, which is Immediate MDT M2.

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 clauses 5.1.1.2 and 5.4.1.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/); 3GPP TS 38.215 V18.4.0 clause 5.1.5 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.215/)

</details>

**063.** What must a UE at least log when it is in the "any cell selection" state during Logged MDT?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　The indicator anyCellSelectionDetected, a time stamp, and the available location information.

**詳解**　TS 37.320 clause 5.1.1.2 states that in "any cell selection" state the UE shall log available information, i.e. at least the anyCellSelectionDetected indicator, time stamp and available location information. In "camped on any cell" state the periodic logging stops while the duration timer keeps running.

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 clause 5.1.1.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/)

</details>

**064.** What is the most consistent interpretation of the records?

1. An overshoot problem, because UEs in G8 and G7 see the same cell
2. Overlapping coverage, because two cells (NP-0610-1 and NP-0610-2) appear in the log
3. G7 is a coverage-hole candidate: weak camped samples preceded repeated no-cell-selection events.
4. A capacity problem, because many UEs were in the mall in the evening

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　3

**詳解**　anyCellSelectionDetected means the UE found no suitable cell; four UEs did so in G7 within two hours, after weak camped samples, near a new building, which fits the TS 37.320 Annex A description of coverage holes caused by obstructions such as new buildings. Option 1 has no distance evidence. Option 2 is wrong because the two cells are logged in different places (G7/G8 versus G2), not as comparable cells at one spot. Option 4 is wrong because idle UEs losing all suitable cells is not a load symptom.

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 clause 5.1.1.2 and Annex A (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/); Curriculum 6.1.1

</details>

**065.** Where were UE-17 and UE-22 when they entered any cell selection?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Cannot be determined: their records contain only the indicator and a time stamp without location information; location would have to come from another source (e.g. later records with location or other location-tagged data for the same time).

**詳解**　TS 37.320 logs "the available location information"; availability depends on UE capability and implementation, so some records carry no location. These two records therefore cannot be assigned to G7 and must not be counted as part of the mall cluster.

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 clauses 4.1 (location information is subject to UE capability/implementation) and 5.1.1.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/)

</details>

**情境 015**　Reviewing a draft Logged MDT job to confirm the L1 hole

A Telco-X (fictional) engineer drafted the job below to confirm the underpass hole. You are asked to review it.

```
Job MDT-PLAN-12 (draft)
1. Type: Logged MDT, management-based, cells NP-0702-1 / NP-0702-2
2. Logged quantities requested: SS-RSRP, SS-RSRQ, SS-SINR
3. Triggers: periodic logging + event L1 (RSRP below threshold) + out-of-coverage detection
4. Expected output: periodic entries with location also from UEs in "camped on any cell" state
5. If not retrieved, UEs keep the logs for 7 days after the logging duration ends
6. Retrieval: when the UE indicates log availability in RRCSetupComplete / RRCResumeComplete
7. Logging duration: (to be decided)
Planned activation: 2026-11-26 18:00, analysis window 18:00-22:00
```

**071.** Which line of the draft is consistent with TS 37.320?

1. Line 2
2. Line 4
3. Line 5
4. Line 6

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　4

**詳解**　TS 37.320 clause 5.1.1.3.1 says an NR UE configured for Logged MDT indicates the availability of logged measurements in RRCSetupComplete or RRCResumeComplete (and in re-establishment/reconfiguration), and the network may then retrieve them. Line 2 is wrong because NR logged quantities are fixed as RSRP and RSRQ. Line 4 is wrong because in "camped on any cell" state periodic logging stops. Line 5 is wrong because non-retrieved logs should be stored for 48 hours after the duration timer expires.

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 clauses 5.1.1.2 and 5.1.1.3.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/)

</details>

**072.** What is wrong with line 3?

1. Periodic logging cannot be configured in NR
2. The out-of-coverage detection trigger only exists for E-UTRA
3. Only one event type can be configured at a time.
4. The L1 event cannot have a threshold

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　3

**詳解**　TS 37.320 clause 5.1.1.1.1 supports two event types for E-UTRAN and NR (measurement-quantity event L1 with configurable threshold, hysteresis and time to trigger, and out-of-coverage detection) and notes that only one type of event can be configured; event-based and periodical logging can be configured independently. Option 1 is wrong because NR supports periodic logging. Option 2 is wrong because both event types are listed "for E-UTRAN and NR". Option 4 contradicts the configurable threshold.

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 clause 5.1.1.1.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/)

</details>

**073.** How long should a UE keep Logged MDT measurements that the network has not retrieved?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　48 hours from the moment the logging duration timer expired.

**詳解**　TS 37.320 clause 5.1.1.3.1: if the network does not retrieve the logs, the UE should store them for 48 hours from expiry of the duration timer, with no requirement beyond 48 hours; all logs and configuration are removed at switch off or detach. If UE memory reserved for MDT fills up, the UE stops logging and starts the 48-hour timer (clause 5.1.1.2).

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 clauses 5.1.1.2 and 5.1.1.3.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/)

</details>

**074.** How should line 2 be corrected?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Remove SS-SINR: for NR the logged downlink quantities are fixed as RSRP and RSRQ (plus beam-level values), so the line should request only SS-RSRP and SS-RSRQ.

**詳解**　TS 37.320 clause 5.1.1.2 fixes the NR logged quantities as both RSRP and RSRQ, and SS-SINR is defined only for RRC_CONNECTED in TS 38.215, so idle-mode logs cannot provide it. If SINR is needed, it must come from connected-mode data such as Immediate MDT M1 or MR.NRScSSSINR.BinX.

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 clause 5.1.1.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/); 3GPP TS 38.215 V18.4.0 clause 5.1.5 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.215/)

</details>

**075.** If the job is activated at 18:00, will the UEs still be logging at 22:00?

1. Cannot determine; duration is unset, and MDT memory may fill first.
2. Yes, because Logged MDT logs until the network retrieves the data
3. No, because Logged MDT always stops when the UE changes RRC state
4. Yes, because the logging duration is fixed at 4 hours by the specification

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　1

**詳解**　The logging duration is a configured timer started at configuration and running independently of state changes; when it expires logging stops. Its value is missing from the draft, and TS 37.320 also stops logging when the UE memory reserved for MDT is full. Option 2 is wrong because logging is bounded by the logging duration timer, not by retrieval; a retrieval before the duration ends only deletes the reported results while logging continues. Option 3 contradicts the timer continuing independently of state changes. Option 4 invents a fixed value.

**出處**　3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 clauses 5.1.1.1.1, 5.1.1.2 and 5.1.1.3.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/)

</details>

#### Ruling out SON before blaming hardware or people: CCO and coverage configuration changes

**情境 016**　Attribute-change notifications and a sudden coverage change

Telco-X (fictional) investigates why cell KH-0420-1 suddenly hands over to a distant cell. The OSS stored the decoded notifications below; KPIs are busy-hour values (SCS 30 kHz, planned radius 900 m, fictional planning value).

```
notifyMOIAttributeValueChanges (decoded), 2026-11-19
02:15:07  object: CommonBeamformingFunction of the sector carrier of KH-0420-1
          attributeValueChange: digitalTilt  newValue=10  oldValue=40
          sourceIndicator: SON_operation
10:02:44  object: NRSectorCarrier of KH-0420-3
          attributeValueChange: configuredMaxTxPower  newValue=20000  oldValue=40000
          sourceIndicator: Management_operation

KH-0420-1 busy hour      11-18     11-20
TA index p90             18        29
SS-RSRP<-115dBm share    12%        6%
MM.HoExeInterReq -> RV-0901-2 (third tier, 3.6 km)   0    85
```

**076.** What change was made to KH-0420-1, and by what kind of process?

1. The downtilt was increased by 3 degrees by an operator command
2. A SON process reduced digital downtilt from 4° to 1°.
3. The tilt was changed from 40° to 10° by a SON process
4. The digital azimuth was turned by 30 units by a management operation

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　2

**詳解**　In TS 28.541 digitalTilt is in units of 0.1 degree and a positive value gives downwards tilt, so 40 → 10 means 4.0° → 1.0° downtilt; sourceIndicator SON_operation means the notification was generated as a result of a SON process. Option 1 reverses the direction and the source. Option 3 ignores the 0.1-degree unit. Option 4 names the wrong attribute and source; Management_operation belongs to the KH-0420-3 power change.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.541 V18.15.0 clause 4.4.1 (digitalTilt) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.541/); 3GPP TS 28.532 V18.8.0 clause 11.1.1.9.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.532/)

</details>

**077.** Which values does TS 28.532 define for the sourceIndicator parameter of notifyMOIAttributeValueChanges?

1. SON_operation and Operator_operation only
2. Automatic, Manual and Unknown
3. Resource_operation, Management_operation and SON_operation, without an "unknown" value
4. Resource_operation, Management_operation, SON_operation and Unknown

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　4

**詳解**　TS 28.532 clause 11.1.1.9.2 defines sourceIndicator as ENUM(Resource_operation, Management_operation, SON_operation, Unknown): internal resource operation, management operation across the managed object boundary, SON process, or source not determinable. Options 1 and 2 invent values. Option 3 omits Unknown.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.532 V18.8.0 clause 11.1.1.9.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.532/)

</details>

**078.** The KH-0420-3 power change carries sourceIndicator = Management_operation. Does this prove that no SON function was involved? Why?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　No; TS 28.532 remarks that a provisioning MnS provider may not always be aware that a SON operation led to the notification, in which case a value other than SON_operation might be sent.

**詳解**　The remark under sourceIndicator in clause 11.1.1.9.2 explicitly allows SON-caused changes to appear with another value. The indicator is therefore a strong hint when it says SON_operation but not proof of absence when it says something else; the SON function's own records must also be checked.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.532 V18.8.0 clause 11.1.1.9.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.532/)

</details>

**079.** How should the KPI change of KH-0420-1 be read together with the notification?

1. The SON uptilt improved weak coverage but suggests possible overshoot.
2. The RU of KH-0420-1 degraded on 11-19, which caused the distant handovers
3. The power reduction on KH-0420-3 caused KH-0420-1's TA to grow
4. Nothing needs to be done, because the low-RSRP share improved

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　1

**詳解**　18 × 39.0625 ≈ 703 m and 29 × 39.0625 ≈ 1,133 m; the TA growth, the new far handovers and the halved low-RSRP share all follow from raising the beam at 02:15. Option 2 ignores the notification and an RU degradation would shrink, not extend, coverage. Option 3 is possible in principle for nearby areas but does not explain handovers 3.6 km away and starts eight hours after the tilt change. Option 4 ignores the side effect on mobility and interference.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.213 V18.9.0 clause 4.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.213/); 3GPP TS 28.313 V18.2.0 clause 8.3.4 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.313/); Curriculum 6.1.1 (rule out SON first)

</details>

**080.** Was the new digitalTilt value of KH-0420-1 inside the range the CCO policy allows? What is missing to decide?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Cannot be determined: the applicable CCO policy (the digitalTiltRange of the CCOParameters subclass object configured for this cell) is not in the data and must be read.

**詳解**　TS 28.313 Table 7.2.3.2.2-1 says the CCO policy indicates the range of the parameters that can be adjusted, referring to the attributes of the abstract class CCOParameters in TS 28.541, which include digitalTiltRange. Without that value the 1.0° setting cannot be checked.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.313 V18.2.0 Table 7.2.3.2.2-1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.313/); 3GPP TS 28.541 V18.15.0 clause 4.3.74 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.541/)

</details>

**情境 017**　A neighbour gNB switches its coverage configuration

NorthLink Mobile (fictional) gNB-B serves cell PL-0310-2 next to gNB-A. The excerpt is a decoded Xn trace summary at gNB-B (field labels simplified) plus gNB-B's handover counts.

```
Xn trace at gNB-B, 2026-11-21 (decoded summary)
03:00:12  NG-RAN NODE CONFIGURATION UPDATE from gNB-A
          cells/SSBs with modified coverage:
            PL-0303-1   coverage state indicator = 5
            PL-0303-2   coverage state indicator = 5 ; SSB index 6: coverage state indicator = 2
            PL-0303-3   planned reconfiguration ; replacing cells: PL-0303-1, PL-0303-2
03:20:00  PL-0303-3 indicated as inactive

gNB-B, source cell PL-0310-2, 03:20 - 06:00
target        handover attempts   successful
PL-0303-3     37                  0   (all preparations failed)
PL-0303-1     112                 109
```

**081.** What does the update from gNB-A indicate?

1. gNB-A has lost its NG connection and all three cells are down
2. gNB-A is asking gNB-B to change gNB-B's own coverage configuration
3. gNB-A changed two coverage configurations and planned a third cell replacement.
4. PL-0303-3 has an RU hardware failure reported over Xn

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　3

**詳解**　TS 38.300 clause 15.5.5.3 says an NG-RAN node may autonomously adjust within and switch between coverage configurations and may notify neighbours with NG-RAN NODE CONFIGURATION UPDATE carrying the modified cells/SSBs with coverage state indicators, possibly with planned reconfiguration and replacing cells. Option 1 is wrong because the cells are still listed with coverage states and two are replacing cells. Option 2 is wrong because the receiving node "may" use the indicator to adopt matching configurations, but the message is a notification, not a request. Option 4 is not what this message reports.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.5.5.3 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)

</details>

**082.** How should gNB-B's 37 handover attempts towards PL-0303-3 after 03:20 be judged?

1. They are correct behaviour, because handovers must continue until the X2/Xn neighbour relation is deleted
2. gNB-B should avoid handovers to an indicated inactive cell; check its handling.
3. They violate a "shall" requirement in TS 38.300, so gNB-B is non-compliant and must be replaced
4. They prove that PL-0303-3 is overshooting

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　2

**詳解**　Clause 15.5.5.3 states that the receiving NG-RAN node should avoid triggering handovers towards cells indicated to be inactive, and may use the information to avoid connection or re-establishment failures. The 37 failed preparations show gNB-B did not do so. Option 1 contradicts that text (and X2 is the LTE interface). Option 3 turns "should" into "shall" and overreacts. Option 4 is wrong because an inactive cell does not radiate coverage at all.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.5.5.3 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)

</details>

**083.** Do PL-0303-1 and PL-0303-2 now cover the whole former area of PL-0303-3 without holes? Answer from the data.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Cannot be determined: the data only show signalling and gNB-B's handover counts; RLF reports, Logged MDT (e.g. anyCellSelectionDetected) or RSRP data from the former PL-0303-3 area after 03:20 are needed.

**詳解**　The update says which cells replace PL-0303-3, not how well they cover its area. The 109/112 handover successes towards PL-0303-1 say nothing about locations that no cell reaches; coverage-hole evidence comes from RLF/MDT data at those locations.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.5.5.3 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/); 3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 clauses 5.1.1.2 and 5.4.1.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/)

</details>

**084.** According to TS 38.300 clause 15.5.5.2, what is the OAM's role for NR coverage and capacity optimisation in the NG-RAN node?

1. OAM must approve each coverage change in real time before the node executes it
2. OAM only collects RLF reports; the node defines its own coverage configurations
3. OAM sends NG-RAN NODE CONFIGURATION UPDATE messages to the neighbour nodes
4. OAM may configure alternative coverage profiles and allowable radio-parameter ranges.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　4

**詳解**　Clause 15.5.5.2 states that each NG-RAN node may be configured with alternative coverage configurations by OAM, containing relevant radio parameters and possibly an allowed adjustment range per parameter; the node may then switch autonomously (15.5.5.3). Option 1 contradicts the autonomous switching. Option 2 reverses who defines the configurations. Option 3 is wrong because the node itself notifies its neighbours over Xn.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clauses 15.5.5.2 and 15.5.5.3 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)

</details>

**085.** What was the handover success rate from PL-0310-2 to PL-0303-1 between 03:20 and 06:00?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　About 97.3%.

**詳解**　109 / 112 ≈ 0.9732, i.e. 97.3%. The replacing cell accepts handovers normally, unlike the inactive PL-0303-3 (0 of 37).

**出處**　Curriculum 6.1.1; 3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.6.1.7 and 5.1.1.6.1.8 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**情境 018**　A centralized CCO run that did not fix the problem

Telco-X (fictional) exported the record of its centralized SON CCO function for cell GE-0711-2 (tool export format is the operator's own; digitalTilt in 0.1 degree units).

```
CCOFunction (scope gNB GE-0711): cCOControl = TRUE
Policy: CCOWeakCoverageParameters, digitalTiltRange = [20, 80]

11-17  detection: weak coverage GE-0711-2, SSB beams 5-7
       inputs: L1M.SS-RSRP.Bin, RLF reports
11-17  action: digitalTilt 60 -> 30   (notification received, sourceIndicator SON_operation)
11-19  evaluation: SS-RSRP<-115dBm share 19% -> 17% ; RLF reports from beams 5-7: 41 -> 38
11-19  action: digitalTilt 30 -> 20   (notification received, sourceIndicator SON_operation)
11-21  evaluation: 17% -> 16% ; RLF reports 38 -> 39
11-21  status: "issue not mitigated - restoring"
11-21  action: digitalTilt 20 -> 60   (notification received, sourceIndicator SON_operation)

Field note 11-23: a 14-storey building completed in October stands in the direction of beams 5-7
```

**086.** What is the most consistent explanation of why the CCO actions had little effect?

1. A new building may block beams 5–7; permitted tilt changes had little effect.
2. The C-SON function malfunctioned, because it restored the original tilt
3. The tilt range was exceeded, so the gNB ignored the commands
4. The RLF reports are wrong, because RLF reports cannot be used for CCO

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　1

**詳解**　Two steps of uptilt down to the policy minimum (20 = 2.0°) improved the low-RSRP share by only 3 points and RLF reports did not fall; a new building in the beam direction matches the "physical obstructions such as new buildings" cause of coverage holes in TS 37.320 Annex A. Option 2 is wrong because restoring when issues are not mitigated is foreseen by TS 28.313. Option 3 is wrong because all values (60, 30, 20) are within [20, 80] and each change was confirmed by a notification. Option 4 is wrong because TS 28.313 lists RLF reports as CCO input.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.313 V18.2.0 clauses 6.4.2.4 and 8.3.4 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.313/); 3GPP TS 37.320 V18.5.0 Annex A (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/37_series/37.320/)

</details>

**087.** Which inputs does TS 28.313 name for the centralized CCO function to detect capacity and coverage issues?

1. Only alarms from the gNB
2. Only drive-test files uploaded by engineers
3. Performance measurements plus MDT, RLF and RCEF reports.
4. Customer complaints from the CRM system and trouble tickets

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　3

**詳解**　TS 28.313 clause 6.4.2.4 (step 1) and clause 8.3.4 (steps 1–3) state that the C-SON function collects measurements (e.g. distribution of RSRP, RSRQ, …) and MDT, RLF and RCEF reports, listed in clauses 7.2.3.3.1 and 7.2.3.3.2. Options 1, 2 and 4 name sources that are not listed.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.313 V18.2.0 clauses 6.4.2.4, 7.2.3.3 and 8.3.4 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.313/)

</details>

**088.** According to TS 28.313, what may the C-SON CCO function do if its evaluation shows that the issues have not been mitigated?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　It may re-configure or restore the CCO control parameters.

**詳解**　The CCO use case in clause 6.4.2.4 (step 5) says the C-SON function may re-configure or restore the CCO control parameters if the issues have not been mitigated; the procedure in clause 8.3.4 (steps 9–10) repeats modifyMOIAttributes followed by a notification with sourceIndicator = SON_operation.
